#12082 1 2006-11-03T20:35:32Z Mysterypea
Stepmania

Stepmania is the game where DDR is in computer. You can make songs. Edit the BPM ( Beat per minute ) And enter in contests. This is good news for mac users. It's available in this version.

Current version is Stepmania 4 CVS. Since it doesn't work i use Stepmania 3.9...
And you download those songs on song downloading. Steps will already be there.
And if you use FlashFlashRevolution
There will be a different step then in the official site. ( THIS IS ONLY WHEN YOU DOWNLOAD FLASHFLASHREVOLUTION FILES )



Step 1- Download the file. ( don't open it )
Step 2- Open the file.
Step 3- Click on the icon that has a paper with an arrow ( > ) in it. Press export packages after then.
Step 4- Click Open Program files. ( The file must always be opened )
Step 5- (Optional) Create a file. If you already did. Ignore this step
Step 6- Now, you need to transfer the file.
Step 6a- You must IMPORTANTLY transfer the document. Transfering the page with an arrow will affect these -> Background And Banner, Song will not be there.
Step 6b- Transfer in not a music document. But in the song pack documents.
Step 7- If the song doesn't work. Mail me.
Step 8- enjoy!

Okay. Here's the jig. It's the competition about arts. There will be a subject each week for an art.
Simple. Just respect the theme and you're done. Here are the rules.

RULES
-Respect the theme of each week. You can add more stuff if you want.
-Winners of the week get to choose the next theme.
-Expecting judges? You are the judge! Vote on the best art you saw.
-The more the votes. More the winning.
-Tileset is allowed. But YOU MUST at least put object art
-You can only sumbit once each week.
-You vote. You can't change. Choose wisely.

Winnings
-First prize: Gets to choose the next theme.
-Other contestants. A big thanks for entering

We'll start with week 1 today. Limit sumbission is next wendsday.

Week one
Bombtastic!
